diff --git a/openspec/changes/per-step-log-fetching/design.md b/openspec/changes/per-step-log-fetching/design.md
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..29aacc40
--- /dev/null
+++ b/openspec/changes/per-step-log-fetching/design.md
@@ -0,0 +1,75 @@
+## Context
+
+The Jenkins capture module currently fetches two levels of log data per build:
+1. **Full build console** — the complete concatenated output (~2.87 MB for a 63-minute build)
+2. **Per-stage console logs** — each stage's output as a separate file (~18.84 MB across 27 stages)
+
+It also fetches per-stage `steps.json` which lists step metadata (id, displayName, result, duration, log URL) but does NOT fetch the actual log content for individual steps.
+
+The Blue Ocean API exposes per-step logs at `/nodes/{node_id}/steps/{step_id}/log/` — plain text, lightweight, typically 0.1–50 KB. These are already referenced in every step's `actions[0]._links.self.href` but never downloaded.
+
+This design addresses adding selective per-step log fetching to close the artifact-attribution and error-diagnosis gaps identified in the `jenkins-inspection-analysis.md` deep-dive.
+
+## Goals / Non-Goals
+
+**Goals:**
+- Fetch individual step logs for steps that are diagnostically valuable (failed, unstable, archive, error-signal)
+- Store them in a flat, de-duplicated layout that handles Blue Ocean's step-bubbling behavior
+- Keep the additional capture size and API cost negligible (<1% of current totals)
+- Maintain backward compatibility — existing captures without `step_logs/` remain valid
+
+**Non-Goals:**
+- Fetching ALL step logs (prohibitive: ~2,400 steps per build)
+- Parsing step log content at capture time (that's a consumer concern)
+- Changing the existing stage-level console log capture
+- Correlating artifacts to stages at capture time (this enables it; consumers implement the correlation)
+
+## Decisions
+
+### Decision 1: Selective fetch with configurable criteria (not all steps)
+
+**Choice:** Fetch logs only for steps matching a fixed set of criteria (failed, unstable, archive, shell-in-failed-stage, error-signal), controlled by a `StepLogFetchConfig` model.
+
+**Alternatives considered:**
+- *Fetch all step logs*: ~2,400 requests per build, ~50 MB additional storage, 10–30s added latency. Disproportionate cost for marginal benefit — 95% of step logs are single-line "Print Message" output already visible in the stage console.
+- *Fetch only failed steps*: Misses the archive-attribution use case entirely and the UNSTABLE diagnosis case.
+
+**Rationale:** The 5 criteria capture the 3 known use cases (error diagnosis, artifact attribution, unstable root cause) while keeping request count at ~47 per build.
+
+### Decision 2: Flat `step_logs/{node_id}_{step_id}.log` layout
+
+**Choice:** Single flat directory with composite filename encoding the true owning node.
+
+**Alternatives considered:**
+- *Nested under stage directories* (`stages/{name}/step_logs/{step_id}.log`): Breaks when Blue Ocean bubbles post-action steps into every ancestor stage. A step that truly belongs to node 15 (Init/Post Actions) would appear under `stages/Docker_Build/step_logs/` — misleading.
+- *Nested under true-owner stage* (resolve owner first, then nest): Requires a lookup pass correlating node_ids to stage names before writing — adds complexity for no real consumer benefit over flat.
+
+**Rationale:** Flat layout avoids the bubbling trap entirely. The `{node_id}` prefix lets consumers filter by stage (`step_logs/3239_*.log` = all step logs for "Start Barrier Workload") without directory nesting.
+
+### Decision 3: De-duplicate by step_id before fetching
+
+**Choice:** After selecting candidate steps across all stages, de-duplicate by `step_id` so each step's log is fetched exactly once, regardless of how many stages Blue Ocean bubbles it into.
+
+**Rationale:** Steps 3335 and 3360 in the analyzed build appear in all 27 stage directories. Without de-duplication, we'd fetch 54 redundant copies. The true owner is extracted from `actions[0]._links.self.href` and used as the `{node_id}` in the filename.
+
+### Decision 4: Parallel fetch with global cap
+
+**Choice:** Fetch step logs with 10-way parallelism, capped at `max_step_logs_per_build=100` total.
+
+**Rationale:** Blue Ocean's log endpoint is lightweight (plain text, no server-side JSON assembly). 10-way parallelism completes ~47 requests in ~0.5s. The 100-step cap prevents runaway fetching on matrix builds with hundreds of failures.
+
+### Decision 5: 1 MB per-step size cap with truncation
+
+**Choice:** Truncate any individual step log at 1 MB.
+
+**Rationale:** Shell script steps in test stages can produce unbounded output (e.g., full pytest/junit output). 1 MB preserves the first ~20K lines which is sufficient for diagnosis while preventing a single step from dominating capture size.
+
+## Risks / Trade-offs
+
+| Risk | Mitigation |
+|------|-----------|
+| Blue Ocean API rate limits on high-parallelism fetch | 10-way parallelism is conservative; can be reduced to 5 via config. Jenkins Blue Ocean has no documented rate limit for read-only endpoints. |
+| Step log endpoint returns 404 for completed builds after Jenkins restart | Capture happens within minutes of build completion (triggered by webhook). Historical builds are already captured. Accept that re-capture of old builds may miss step logs. |
+| `displayName` matching is brittle (locale, plugin version) | Use exact English strings from Blue Ocean; document known values. If a future Jenkins version changes step names, the criteria miss those steps silently — they're additive, not destructive. |
+| `node_id` extraction from href depends on URL format stability | The `/nodes/{id}/steps/{id}/log/` pattern is part of Blue Ocean's REST contract since 2017. Pin to regex extraction with a fallback that logs a warning and skips the step. |
+| Truncated step logs lose tail content | Truncation preserves the head (where the command/setup is). For tail-important cases (final error line), the failed-step and error-signal criteria overlap — the error signal step itself is short and won't be truncated. |
diff --git a/openspec/changes/per-step-log-fetching/proposal.md b/openspec/changes/per-step-log-fetching/proposal.md
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..858a7387
--- /dev/null
+++ b/openspec/changes/per-step-log-fetching/proposal.md
@@ -0,0 +1,28 @@
+## Why
+
+The current capture plan fetches stage-level console logs but not individual step logs. This leaves three blind spots: (1) `Archive the artifacts` steps have empty `displayDescription`, so artifact→stage attribution requires reading each archive step's log to learn its `includes:` pattern; (2) when a step fails, the stage-level console mixes its output with hundreds of unrelated steps — the step-level log isolates the exact failure; (3) UNSTABLE root causes (Checkmarx/Sonar/hadolint) are buried in megabyte-sized stage logs instead of being directly addressable.
+
+## What Changes
+
+- Add selective per-step log fetching to the Jenkins capture plan — fetch logs only for steps matching configurable criteria (failed, unstable, archive, shell-in-failed-stage, error-signal)
+- Introduce a `step_logs/` directory in the HDLF capture layout with flat `{node_id}_{step_id}.log` naming
+- Extend `manifest.json` with `type: "step_log"` entries including `fetch_reason` for traceability
+- Add `StepLogFetchConfig` to control selection criteria, max count, and max size per step log
+- De-duplicate bubbled steps (Blue Ocean's post-action step bubbling) using the true node_id from the log href
+
+## Capabilities
+
+### New Capabilities
+
+- `step-log-capture`: Selective fetching, storage, and manifest registration of per-step Jenkins Blue Ocean logs during pipeline inspection capture
+
+### Modified Capabilities
+
+- `ingestion-api`: No requirement changes — this is purely additive to the capture workflow internals
+
+## Impact
+
+- **Code**: Jenkins capture module (step selection logic, parallel HTTP fetch, manifest writer)
+- **Storage**: ~110 KB additional per build (<0.5% increase on a 33.7 MB capture)
+- **APIs consumed**: Blue Ocean `/nodes/{node_id}/steps/{step_id}/log/` endpoint (~47 additional requests per failed build, parallelizable)
+- **Backward compatibility**: Additive-only — existing captures remain valid; consumers check for `step_logs/` existence
diff --git a/openspec/changes/per-step-log-fetching/specs/step-log-capture/spec.md b/openspec/changes/per-step-log-fetching/specs/step-log-capture/spec.md
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..40cc9bcb
--- /dev/null
+++ b/openspec/changes/per-step-log-fetching/specs/step-log-capture/spec.md
@@ -0,0 +1,99 @@
+## ADDED Requirements
+
+### Requirement: Select steps for log fetching based on diagnostic criteria
+The capture module SHALL select steps for individual log fetching based on configurable criteria. A step SHALL be selected when any of the following conditions are true: (1) the step's `result` is `FAILURE`; (2) the step's `result` is `UNSTABLE`; (3) the step's `displayName` equals `"Archive the artifacts"`; (4) the step's `displayName` equals `"Shell Script"` AND the step belongs to a stage whose `result` is `FAILURE`; (5) the step's `displayName` equals `"Error signal"`. Each criterion SHALL be independently togglable via configuration.
+
+#### Scenario: Failed step selected
+- **WHEN** a step has `result == "FAILURE"` and `fetch_failed` is enabled
+- **THEN** the step SHALL be selected for log fetching with `fetch_reason = "failed_step"`
+
+#### Scenario: Unstable step selected
+- **WHEN** a step has `result == "UNSTABLE"` and `fetch_unstable` is enabled
+- **THEN** the step SHALL be selected for log fetching with `fetch_reason = "unstable_step"`
+
+#### Scenario: Archive step selected regardless of result
+- **WHEN** a step has `displayName == "Archive the artifacts"` and `fetch_archive` is enabled
+- **THEN** the step SHALL be selected for log fetching with `fetch_reason = "archive_step"`
+
+#### Scenario: Shell script in failed stage selected
+- **WHEN** a step has `displayName == "Shell Script"` AND belongs to a stage with `result == "FAILURE"` and `fetch_shell_in_failed_stage` is enabled
+- **THEN** the step SHALL be selected for log fetching with `fetch_reason = "shell_in_failed_stage"`
+
+#### Scenario: Error signal step selected
+- **WHEN** a step has `displayName == "Error signal"` and `fetch_error_signal` is enabled
+- **THEN** the step SHALL be selected for log fetching with `fetch_reason = "error_signal"`
+
+#### Scenario: Criterion disabled skips matching steps
+- **WHEN** a step matches a criterion but that criterion is disabled in configuration (e.g., `fetch_archive = false`)
+- **THEN** the step SHALL NOT be selected for log fetching under that criterion
+
+### Requirement: De-duplicate bubbled steps before fetching
+The capture module SHALL de-duplicate selected steps by `step_id` before issuing fetch requests. When the same `step_id` appears in multiple stages (Blue Ocean step bubbling), it SHALL be fetched exactly once. The true owning node SHALL be determined by extracting `node_id` from the step's `actions[0]._links.self.href` URL path (pattern: `/nodes/{node_id}/steps/{step_id}/log/`).
+
+#### Scenario: Bubbled step fetched once with true owner
+- **WHEN** step 3335 appears in all 27 stage step listings but its log href contains `/nodes/15/steps/3335/log/`
+- **THEN** the step log SHALL be fetched exactly once and stored as `step_logs/15_3335.log`
+
+#### Scenario: Non-bubbled step uses its stage's node_id
+- **WHEN** step 3272 appears only in stage node 3239's step listing and its log href contains `/nodes/3239/steps/3272/log/`
+- **THEN** the step log SHALL be stored as `step_logs/3239_3272.log`
+
+### Requirement: Fetch step logs via Blue Ocean REST API
+The capture module SHALL fetch each selected step's log from the Blue Ocean endpoint at `/blue/rest/organizations/jenkins/pipelines/{pipeline}/branches/{branch}/runs/{build}/nodes/{node_id}/steps/{step_id}/log/`. The response SHALL be treated as plain text. Requests SHALL be parallelized with a configurable concurrency limit (default: 10).
+
+#### Scenario: Successful step log fetch
+- **WHEN** the Blue Ocean log endpoint returns HTTP 200 with plain text content
+- **THEN** the content SHALL be written to `step_logs/{node_id}_{step_id}.log` and a manifest entry created with `status = "captured"`
+
+#### Scenario: Step log endpoint returns 404
+- **WHEN** the Blue Ocean log endpoint returns HTTP 404 for a selected step
+- **THEN** no file SHALL be written and the manifest entry SHALL have `status = "skipped_unreachable"`
+
+#### Scenario: Step log exceeds size cap
+- **WHEN** a step log response exceeds `max_step_log_size_bytes` (default: 1,048,576 bytes)
+- **THEN** the content SHALL be truncated at the configured limit and the manifest entry SHALL include `truncated = true`
+
+### Requirement: Enforce global cap on step log count
+The capture module SHALL NOT fetch more than `max_step_logs_per_build` step logs per build (default: 100). When the selection criteria produce more candidates than the cap, steps SHALL be prioritized in this order: (1) failed steps, (2) error signals, (3) shell scripts in failed stages, (4) unstable steps, (5) archive steps. Excess steps beyond the cap SHALL be silently dropped.
+
+#### Scenario: Cap not exceeded
+- **WHEN** selection criteria produce 47 candidate steps and `max_step_logs_per_build = 100`
+- **THEN** all 47 step logs SHALL be fetched
+
+#### Scenario: Cap exceeded with priority ordering
+- **WHEN** selection criteria produce 120 candidate steps and `max_step_logs_per_build = 100`
+- **THEN** the 100 highest-priority steps SHALL be fetched (failed > error_signal > shell_in_failed_stage > unstable > archive)
+- **AND** the remaining 20 steps SHALL not be fetched
+
+### Requirement: Store step logs in flat directory with composite filename
+Step logs SHALL be stored in a `step_logs/` directory at the root of the HDLF capture folder. Each file SHALL be named `{node_id}_{step_id}.log` where `node_id` is the true owning node extracted from the step's log href. The directory SHALL NOT use subdirectories.
+
+#### Scenario: Step log stored with correct filename
+- **WHEN** a step log is fetched for step_id 3272 belonging to node_id 3239
+- **THEN** the file SHALL be written to `step_logs/3239_3272.log`
+
+#### Scenario: Consumer filters step logs by stage
+- **WHEN** a consumer wants all step logs for stage node 3239
+- **THEN** the consumer can glob `step_logs/3239_*.log` to retrieve them
+
+### Requirement: Register step logs in manifest with fetch metadata
+Each fetched step log SHALL produce an entry in `manifest.json` with `type = "step_log"`. The entry SHALL include: `path`, `step_id`, `node_id`, `stage_name` (display name of the owning stage), `display_name` (step's displayName), `result` (step's result), `fetch_reason`, `captured_size`, and `status`. When truncated, the entry SHALL additionally include `truncated = true`.
+
+#### Scenario: Manifest entry for captured step log
+- **WHEN** step 3272 (Shell Script, FAILURE) in stage "Start Barrier Workload" (node 3239) is fetched successfully at 4,821 bytes
+- **THEN** the manifest entry SHALL be `{"type": "step_log", "path": "step_logs/3239_3272.log", "step_id": "3272", "node_id": "3239", "stage_name": "Start Barrier Workload", "display_name": "Shell Script", "result": "FAILURE", "fetch_reason": "failed_step", "captured_size": 4821, "status": "captured"}`
+
+#### Scenario: Manifest entry for unreachable step log
+- **WHEN** the log endpoint for step 3275 returns HTTP 404
+- **THEN** the manifest entry SHALL have `"status": "skipped_unreachable"` and `"captured_size": 0`
+
+### Requirement: Backward-compatible capture format
+The `step_logs/` directory SHALL be purely additive to the existing HDLF capture layout. Builds captured without step log fetching enabled SHALL remain valid — no existing file paths or manifest structures SHALL change. Consumers SHALL check for `step_logs/` directory existence before attempting to read step logs.
+
+#### Scenario: Existing capture without step logs remains valid
+- **WHEN** a consumer processes an HDLF capture that was created before step log fetching was implemented
+- **THEN** the consumer SHALL not fail due to absence of `step_logs/` directory or `type: "step_log"` manifest entries
+
+#### Scenario: Step log fetching disabled produces no step_logs directory
+- **WHEN** `StepLogFetchConfig.enabled = false`
+- **THEN** no `step_logs/` directory SHALL be created and no `type: "step_log"` manifest entries SHALL be written
diff --git a/openspec/changes/per-step-log-fetching/tasks.md b/openspec/changes/per-step-log-fetching/tasks.md
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..01799abb
--- /dev/null
+++ b/openspec/changes/per-step-log-fetching/tasks.md
@@ -0,0 +1,36 @@
+## 1. Configuration Model
+
+- [ ] 1.1 Define `StepLogFetchConfig` Pydantic model with fields: `enabled`, `fetch_failed`, `fetch_unstable`, `fetch_archive`, `fetch_shell_in_failed_stage`, `fetch_error_signal`, `max_step_logs_per_build`, `max_step_log_size_bytes`
+- [ ] 1.2 Integrate `StepLogFetchConfig` into the capture plan's existing settings/config hierarchy
+
+## 2. Step Selection Logic
+
+- [ ] 2.1 Implement `_classify_step_for_fetch()` — returns the `fetch_reason` string or `None` based on criteria matching
+- [ ] 2.2 Implement `_extract_node_id_from_href()` — parse `/nodes/{node_id}/steps/{step_id}/log/` from `actions[0]._links.self.href`
+- [ ] 2.3 Implement `_select_steps_for_log_fetch()` — iterate all stage steps, classify, de-duplicate by step_id, apply priority ordering, enforce `max_step_logs_per_build` cap
+- [ ] 2.4 Define `StepLogTarget` model with fields: `step_id`, `node_id`, `stage_name`, `display_name`, `result`, `fetch_reason`
+
+## 3. Step Log Fetching
+
+- [ ] 3.1 Implement `_fetch_step_log()` — single HTTP GET to Blue Ocean step log endpoint, handle 200/404/timeout, truncate at `max_step_log_size_bytes`
+- [ ] 3.2 Implement `_fetch_all_step_logs()` — parallel fetch with configurable concurrency (default 10), collect results with success/failure/truncation status
+- [ ] 3.3 Handle HTTP 404 gracefully — produce `status: "skipped_unreachable"` manifest entry without failing the overall capture
+
+## 4. Storage and Manifest
+
+- [ ] 4.1 Write fetched step logs to `step_logs/{node_id}_{step_id}.log` in the HDLF capture directory
+- [ ] 4.2 Generate manifest entries with `type: "step_log"` including all required metadata fields (`path`, `step_id`, `node_id`, `stage_name`, `display_name`, `result`, `fetch_reason`, `captured_size`, `status`, optional `truncated`)
+- [ ] 4.3 Skip `step_logs/` directory creation entirely when `StepLogFetchConfig.enabled = false`
+
+## 5. Integration
+
+- [ ] 5.1 Wire step log fetching into the Jenkins capture pipeline — call after `steps.json` fetching completes (needs step metadata as input)
+- [ ] 5.2 Pass `failed_stage_ids` set (from `pipeline_tree.json` nodes with `result == "FAILURE"`) to the selection logic
+
+## 6. Tests
+
+- [ ] 6.1 Unit test `_classify_step_for_fetch()` — cover all 5 criteria enabled/disabled, edge cases (step matching multiple criteria uses first match)
+- [ ] 6.2 Unit test `_extract_node_id_from_href()` — valid href, malformed href (returns None with warning)
+- [ ] 6.3 Unit test `_select_steps_for_log_fetch()` — de-duplication of bubbled steps, priority ordering when cap exceeded, empty input
+- [ ] 6.4 Unit test `_fetch_step_log()` — mock 200 response, 404 response, truncation at size limit
+- [ ] 6.5 Integration test with real HDLF inspection data — verify against known step counts from `0858e8db` inspection

@@ -0,0 +1,558 @@
+# Unified CI-Agnostic Schema — Architecture Map
+
+## 1. Big Picture: What & Why
+
+```
+┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+│                         THE PROBLEM                                          │
+│                                                                             │
+│   Jenkins ──┐                                                               │
+│   Azure DevOps ──┼──▶ Every consumer has per-provider IF/ELSE branching     │
+│   GitHub Actions ─┘    (HDLF Server, Handler Orchestrator, MCP tools)       │
+└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
+                                    │
+                              SOLUTION ▼
+┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+│                    NORMALIZE AT WRITE-TIME                                   │
+│                                                                             │
+│   Jenkins ──────────┐                                                       │
+│   Azure DevOps ─────┼──▶ UNIFIED FORMAT ──▶ All consumers read ONE shape   │
+│   GitHub Actions ───┘     (at extraction)                                   │
+└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
+```
+
+---
+
+## 2. Data Flow: End-to-End Pipeline
+
+```
+ ┌──────────────────────┐
+ │    CI SYSTEMS         │
+ │  ┌─────────────────┐  │
+ │  │ Jenkins         │  │
+ │  │ Azure DevOps    │  │
+ │  │ GitHub Actions  │  │
+ │  └────────┬────────┘  │
+ └───────────┼───────────┘
+             │ raw API responses
+             ▼
+ ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+ │              INGESTION API                         │
+ │                                                   │
+ │  URL Validation ──▶ source_system detection       │
+ │  • JenkinsUrlValidator      ✓ enabled             │
+ │  • AzureDevOpsUrlValidator  ✓ enabled             │
+ │  • GitHubActionsValidator   ✗ deferred (HTTP 422) │
+ │                                                   │
+ │  ci_system stored in DB ──────────────────────────┼──▶ Inspection table
+ └───────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘
+                     │ source_system in request
+                     ▼
+ ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+ │        TEMPORAL WORKFLOW (existing activities)     │
+ │                                                   │
+ │  extract_metadata_activity ──┐                    │
+ │  extract_data_activity ──────┤ dispatch by        │
+ │                              │ source_system      │
+ │  ┌─────────────────┐  ┌─────────────────┐        │
+ │  │ Jenkins path    │  │ ADO path         │        │
+ │  │ (+ normalizer)  │  │ (new extractor)  │        │
+ │  └────────┬────────┘  └────────┬─────────┘        │
+ └───────────┼────────────────────┼──────────────────┘
+             │                    │
+             ▼                    ▼
+ ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+ │         HDLF INSPECTION FOLDER (unified output)   │
+ │                                                   │
+ │  Written once. Read by ALL consumers identically. │
+ │  (see Section 3 for folder structure)             │
+ └───────────────────────┬───────────────────────────┘
+                         │
+          ┌──────────────┼──────────────────┐
+          ▼              ▼                  ▼
+ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────────┐
+ │ HDLF Server  │ │ MCP Tools    │ │ Handler          │
+ │ (REST API)   │ │ (dual-mode)  │ │ Orchestrator     │
+ │              │ │              │ │                  │
+ │ Uses reader  │ │ Uses reader  │ │ Uses ci_system   │
+ │ protocol     │ │ protocol     │ │ from DB column   │
+ └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────────┘
+```
+
+---
+
+## 3. Inspection Folder Layout
+
+```
+<uuid>/
+│
+├── metadata.json              ◀─── Universal entry point (CI-agnostic)
+├── execution_timeline.json    ◀─── Required aggregate full-run view
+├── pipeline_run.json          ◀─── PipelineRun model (top-level summary)
+├── stages.json                ◀─── Flat array of Stage models
+├── steps/
+│   ├── <stage_id_1>.json      ◀─── Steps within stage 1
+│   └── <stage_id_2>.json      ◀─── Steps within stage 2
+├── jobs.json                  ◀─── JobDetail models (Azure DevOps; [] for Jenkins)
+├── parallel_groups.json       ◀─── ParallelGroup models ([] if no parallelism)
+├── artifacts.json             ◀─── Artifact models with attribution
+├── logs/
+│   ├── console.log            ◀─── Full build log
+│   ├── <stage_id>.log         ◀─── Stage-level log (always present)
+│   └── <stage_id>/
+│       ├── <step_id_1>.log    ◀─── Per-step logs (when has_step_logs=true)
+│       └── <step_id_2>.log
+│
+├── provider_raw/              ◀─── Raw API responses (never read by unified reader)
+│   ├── jenkins/
+│   │   ├── blue_ocean_run.json
+│   │   ├── pipeline_tree.json
+│   │   ├── wfapi_describe.json
+│   │   ├── issues/<stage_id>.json
+│   │   └── step_logs/...
+│   └── azure_devops/
+│       ├── build.json
+│       ├── timeline.json
+│       ├── issues/<stage_id>.json
+│       ├── attempts/<n>/timeline.json
+│       ├── logs/<log_id>.txt
+│       └── artifacts_list.json
+│
+└── manifest.json
+```
+
+---
+
+## 4. Unified Data Models (Pydantic)
+
+```
+┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+│                         ExecutionTimeline                                    │
+│                                                                             │
+│  schema_version, pipeline_run                                               │
+│  stages, steps_by_stage                                                     │
+│  jobs, parallel_groups, artifacts                                           │
+│  provider_extensions                                                        │
+└─────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────┘
+                                      │ aggregates
+                                      ▼
+┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+│                          PipelineRun                                         │
+│                                                                             │
+│  id, ci_system, url, result, state, result_confidence                       │
+│  duration_ms, start_time, end_time                                          │
+│  commit_id, branch, pr_number, pr_title, pr_author, pr_url                  │
+│  build_number (Jenkins compat), run_identifier (universal)                  │
+│  display_name, git_repos, parameters, provider_extensions                   │
+└─────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────┘
+                                      │ contains
+                    ┌─────────────────┼─────────────────┐
+                    ▼                 ▼                  ▼
+┌──────────────────────┐  ┌────────────────────┐  ┌───────────────────┐
+│     Stage            │  │  ParallelGroup     │  │    Artifact       │
+│                      │  │                    │  │                   │
+│  id, display_name    │  │  id, display_name  │  │  file_name        │
+│  type (stage/        │  │  parent_stage_id   │  │  relative_path    │
+│    parallel/job/     │  │  strategy          │  │  mime_type         │
+│    phase)            │  │  legs: [Parallel   │  │  size_bytes        │
+│  result, state       │  │         Leg]       │  │  producing_stage_id│
+│  result_confidence   │  │                    │  │  producing_step_id │
+│  duration_ms         │  └────────────────────┘  │  candidate_stage_  │
+│  parent_stage_id     │                          │    ids             │
+│  depends_on: [ids]   │  ┌────────────────────┐  │  attribution_      │
+│  order               │  │   ParallelLeg      │  │    method          │
+│  ran                 │  │                    │  │  attribution_      │
+│  attempt,            │  │  stage_id          │  │    confidence      │
+│    total_attempts    │  │  display_name      │  │  download_url      │
+│  has_prior_failure   │  │  result            │  │  provider_         │
+│  failure_category    │  │  matrix_values     │  │    extensions      │
+│  failure_messages    │  └────────────────────┘  └───────────────────┘
+│  has_step_logs       │
+│  provider_extensions │
+└──────────┬───────────┘
+           │ contains
+           ▼
+┌──────────────────────┐       ┌──────────────────────┐
+│       Step           │       │     JobDetail        │
+│                      │       │   (Azure DevOps)     │
+│  id, display_name    │       │                      │
+│  display_description │       │  id, display_name    │
+│  result, state       │       │  agent_name          │
+│  duration_ms         │       │  agent_pool          │
+│  start_time          │       │  continue_on_error   │
+│  log_ref             │       │  depends_on          │
+│  job_id (ADO/GHA)    │       │  timeout_minutes     │
+│  is_conditional_     │       │  matrix_leg          │
+│    failure           │       │                      │
+│  provider_extensions │       └──────────────────────┘
+└──────────────────────┘
+```
+
+---
+
+## 5. Status Vocabulary (PipelineStatus Enum)
+
+```
+┌────────────────┐
+│ Unified Status │     Jenkins          Azure DevOps       GitHub Actions
+├────────────────┤     ───────          ──────────────     ──────────────
+│ SUCCESS        │ ←── SUCCESS          succeeded          success
+│ FAILURE        │ ←── FAILURE          failed             failure
+│ UNSTABLE       │ ←── UNSTABLE         partiallySucceeded (inferred*)
+│ ABORTED        │ ←── ABORTED          canceled           cancelled
+│ SKIPPED        │ ←── NOT_BUILT        —                  skipped
+│ IN_PROGRESS    │ ←── (running)        inProgress         in_progress
+│ QUEUED         │ ←── (queued)         notStarted         queued
+│ UNKNOWN        │ ←── (unmapped)       (unmapped)         (unmapped)
+└────────────────┘
+
+* GHA has no native UNSTABLE — inferred from test analysis (result_confidence="inferred")
+```
+
+---
+
+## 6. Reader Architecture (Consumer-Side)
+
+```
+                     ┌──────────────────────────────────────┐
+                     │     InspectionReaderProtocol          │
+                     │                                      │
+                     │  get_execution_timeline(uuid)        │
+                     │  get_pipeline_run(uuid)              │
+                     │  list_stages(uuid)                   │
+                     │  get_stage_steps(uuid, stage_id)     │
+                     │  get_jobs(uuid)                      │
+                     │  get_parallel_groups(uuid)           │
+                     │  list_artifacts(uuid)                │
+                     │  get_artifact_provenance(uuid, path) │
+                     │  stream_log(uuid, stage_id, step_id) │
+                     │  get_metadata(uuid)                  │
+                     │  get_stage_dependencies(uuid, id)    │
+                     │  get_stage_dependents(uuid, id)      │
+                     └────────────────┬─────────────────────┘
+                                      │
+                     ┌────────────────┴───────────────────┐
+                     │                                    │
+          ┌──────────▼──────────┐           ┌────────────▼────────────┐
+          │ UnifiedInspection   │           │ LegacyJenkinsReader     │
+          │ Reader              │           │                         │
+          │                     │           │ Reads old-format        │
+          │ Reads new-format    │           │ inspection folders      │
+          │ JSON files directly │           │ (pipeline_tree.json,    │
+          │                     │           │  wfapi_describe.json)   │
+          │ No CI-specific      │           │ and converts to unified │
+          │ logic               │           │ models at read-time     │
+          └─────────────────────┘           └─────────────────────────┘
+                     ▲                                    ▲
+                     │                                    │
+                     └──────────create_reader()───────────┘
+                                      │
+                          Probes for pipeline_run.json:
+                          • Found → UnifiedInspectionReader
+                          • Missing → LegacyJenkinsReader
+```
+
+---
+
+## 7. Extraction Flow Per CI System
+
+### Jenkins (existing + normalizer)
+```
+Jenkins API ──▶ Blue Ocean + WFAPI downloads ──▶ provider_raw/jenkins/
+                                                         │
+                                                         ▼
+                                                 Jenkins Normalizer
+                                                 (post-processing)
+                                                         │
+                                                         ▼
+                                            Unified JSON files (top-level)
+                                            • execution_timeline.json
+                                            • pipeline_run.json
+                                            • stages.json
+                                            • steps/<id>.json
+                                            • jobs.json  ← [] for Jenkins
+                                            • parallel_groups.json
+                                            • artifacts.json
+                                            • logs/<id>.log
+```
+
+### Azure DevOps (new extractor)
+```
+ADO API ──▶ build.json + timeline.json + logs ──▶ provider_raw/azure_devops/
+                                                          │
+                                                          ▼
+                                                  ADO Normalizer
+                                                  (inline in extractor)
+                                                          │
+                                                          ▼
+                                             Unified JSON files (top-level)
+                                             • execution_timeline.json
+                                             • pipeline_run.json
+                                             • stages.json
+                                             • steps/<id>.json
+                                             • jobs.json  ← Azure DevOps job details
+                                             • parallel_groups.json
+                                             • artifacts.json
+                                             • logs/<id>.log
+```
+
+---
+
+## 8. Stage Hierarchy: Flat Array with Pointers
+
+```
+                                Jenkins
+┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+│  stages.json (flat array)                                  │
+│                                                           │
+│  ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐                    │
+│  │ Stage: Build │     │ Stage: Test  │                    │
+│  │ type="stage" │     │ type="stage" │                    │
+│  │ id="1"       │     │ id="3"       │                    │
+│  │ parent=null  │     │ parent=null  │                    │
+│  └──────────────┘     └──────┬───────┘                    │
+│                              │ parent_stage_id="3"        │
+│                    ┌─────────┴──────────┐                 │
+│                    ▼                    ▼                  │
+│           ┌───────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐           │
+│           │ type="parallel│   │ type="parallel│           │
+│           │ id="4"        │   │ id="5"        │           │
+│           │ "Unit Tests"  │   │ "IT Tests"    │           │
+│           └───────────────┘   └───────────────┘           │
+└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
+
+                          Azure DevOps
+┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+│  stages.json (flat array)                                  │
+│                                                           │
+│  ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐                    │
+│  │ Stage: Build │     │ Stage: Deploy│                    │
+│  │ type="stage" │     │ type="stage" │                    │
+│  │ depends_on=[]│     │ depends_on=  │                    │
+│  │              │     │   ["build"]  │ ◀── DAG deps      │
+│  └──────┬───────┘     └──────────────┘                    │
+│         │ parent_stage_id="build"                         │
+│         ▼                                                 │
+│  ┌──────────────┐                                         │
+│  │ type="job"   │ ◀── intermediate execution unit         │
+│  │ id="build_j1"│                                         │
+│  └──────────────┘                                         │
+└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
+```
+
+---
+
+## 9. Artifact Attribution Cascade
+
+```
+                    HIGH CONFIDENCE
+                    ┌───────────────────────────────────────┐
+                    │ api_source (ADO: "source" field)      │
+                    │ → producing_stage_id ✓                │
+                    │ → producing_step_id ✓                 │
+                    └───────────────┬───────────────────────┘
+                                   │ fallback
+                    MEDIUM-HIGH    ▼
+                    ┌───────────────────────────────────────┐
+                    │ archive_step_node (Jenkins: step      │
+                    │   log href → owning node)             │
+                    │ → producing_stage_id ✓                │
+                    │ → producing_step_id (if unique) ✓     │
+                    └───────────────┬───────────────────────┘
+                                   │ fallback
+                    MEDIUM         ▼
+                    ┌───────────────────────────────────────┐
+                    │ naming_heuristic (artifact dir name   │
+                    │   matches stage display name)         │
+                    │ → producing_stage_id (probable)       │
+                    └───────────────┬───────────────────────┘
+                                   │ fallback
+                    LOW            ▼
+                    ┌───────────────────────────────────────┐
+                    │ mtime_correlation (file timestamp     │
+                    │   within stage time window)           │
+                    │ → producing_stage_id (guess)          │
+                    └───────────────┬───────────────────────┘
+                                   │ fallback
+                    NONE           ▼
+                    ┌───────────────────────────────────────┐
+                    │ Unattributable                         │
+                    │ → all attribution fields = None       │
+                    └───────────────────────────────────────┘
+```
+
+---
+
+## 10. MCP Dual-Mode (Transition Strategy)
+
+```
+┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+│              MCP Tool (e.g. get_stage_log)                  │
+│                                                            │
+│  Incoming request ──▶ Probe: pipeline_run.json exists?     │
+│                              │                │            │
+│                         YES  ▼           NO   ▼            │
+│                  ┌──────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐      │
+│                  │ UNIFIED MODE │   │ LEGACY MODE   │      │
+│                  │              │   │               │      │
+│                  │ Returns:     │   │ Returns:      │      │
+│                  │ • Execution  │   │ • Assembled   │      │
+│                  │   Timeline   │   │   timeline    │      │
+│                  │ • Stage      │   │ • StageSummary│      │
+│                  │ • Step       │   │ • StepInfo    │      │
+│                  │ • Artifact   │   │ • ArtifactEntry│     │
+│                  │              │   │               │      │
+│                  │ Keys: id     │   │ Keys: dir     │      │
+│                  └──────────────┘   └───────────────┘      │
+│                                                            │
+│  Handlers use whichever fields are present:                │
+│  • id (new) OR dir (legacy) for stage identification       │
+│  • stage_id (new) OR stage_dir (legacy) for log requests   │
+└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
+```
+
+---
+
+## 11. Migration Path: Before → After
+
+```
+BEFORE (Jenkins-only)                    AFTER (CI-agnostic)
+─────────────────────                    ───────────────────
+
+<uuid>/                                  <uuid>/
+├── metadata.json                        ├── metadata.json  ← +ci_system, +pipeline_url
+│                                        ├── execution_timeline.json ← NEW required aggregate
+├── pipeline_tree.json ─── MOVED ──▶     ├── pipeline_run.json   ← NEW (unified)
+├── wfapi_describe.json ── MOVED ──▶     ├── stages.json         ← NEW (unified)
+├── steps.json ─────────── MOVED ──▶     ├── steps/<id>.json     ← NEW (per-stage)
+├── artifacts/                           ├── jobs.json            ← NEW (ADO details; [] for Jenkins)
+│   └── ...                              ├── parallel_groups.json ← NEW
+└── logs/                                ├── artifacts.json       ← NEW (unified)
+    └── ...                              ├── logs/                ← restructured
+                                         │   ├── console.log
+                                         │   ├── <stage_id>.log
+                                         │   └── <stage_id>/<step_id>.log
+                                         ├── provider_raw/       ← NEW (raw originals)
+                                         │   └── jenkins/
+                                         │       ├── pipeline_tree.json  (moved here)
+                                         │       ├── wfapi_describe.json (moved here)
+                                         │       └── issues/<id>.json
+                                         └── manifest.json
+
+ LegacyJenkinsReader handles old folders ──┘
+```
+
+---
+
+## 12. Key Design Decisions Summary
+
+```
+┌────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+│ #  │ Decision                                                          │
+├────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
+│ D1 │ Unified JSON files live at top-level alongside metadata.json     │
+│ D2 │ Common status vocabulary (8 values) mapped per-provider          │
+│ D3 │ Raw data isolated in provider_raw/<ci_system>/                   │
+│ D4 │ Single reader protocol; factory picks impl by probing files      │
+│ D5 │ Flat stage array + parent_id/depends_on (not nested trees)       │
+│ D6 │ IDs are strings, provider-assigned, run-scoped                   │
+│ D7 │ All timestamps UTC ISO 8601; logs 1-indexed                      │
+│ D8 │ Artifacts carry attribution with confidence cascade              │
+│ D9 │ MCP tools dual-mode during migration                            │
+│ D10│ metadata.json additive-only evolution (no breaking renames)       │
+│ D11│ GHA URLs rejected (HTTP 422) until extractor is built            │
+│ D12│ CI dispatch inside existing Temporal activities (no new ones)     │
+│ D13│ Per-step logs at logs/<stage_id>/<step_id>.log                   │
+│ D14│ ci_system column on Inspection DB table                          │
+│ D15│ Canonical ci_system = snake_case ("azure_devops" not "Azure DevOps")│
+└────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
+```
+
+---
+
+## 13. Component Impact Map
+
+```
+ ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+ │                        COMPONENTS AFFECTED                                │
+ │                                                                          │
+ │  fl_shared/                                                              │
+ │  ├── unified_models.py ──── PipelineRun, Stage, Step, Artifact, etc. NEW │
+ │  ├── reader_protocol.py ─── InspectionReaderProtocol                 NEW │
+ │  ├── status_mapping.py ──── PipelineStatus enum + per-provider maps  NEW │
+ │  └── metadata_models.py ─── Extended with ci_system, pipeline_url  EDIT  │
+ │                                                                          │
+ │  hdlf_server/                                                            │
+ │  ├── reader.py ────── Rewritten → UnifiedInspectionReader          REWRITE│
+ │  ├── routes.py ────── +provider-raw endpoints, uses create_reader()  EDIT │
+ │  └── models.py ────── CiSystem snake_case migration                 EDIT │
+ │                                                                          │
+ │  fl_control_plane/                                                       │
+ │  ├── data_extractor/ ── Jenkins normalizer + ADO extraction pipeline NEW │
+ │  ├── metadata_extractor/ ── +ci_system, +pipeline_url, +run_id     EDIT  │
+ │  ├── credentials.py ── +get_azure_devops_credentials()             EDIT  │
+ │  ├── ingestion_api/ ── +ADO URL validator, source_system detection  EDIT │
+ │  ├── temporal/ ──────── CI dispatch in existing activities          EDIT  │
+ │  └── database.py ────── +ci_system column, Alembic migration        EDIT │
+ │                                                                          │
+ │  fl_mcp_servers/                                                         │
+ │  └── pipeline_data_mcp.py ── Dual-mode tools (unified + legacy)    EDIT  │
+ │                                                                          │
+ │  Handler Orchestrator ── reads ci_system from DB (minor)           EDIT   │
+ └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
+```
+
+---
+
+## 14. Spec File Relationship Map
+
+```
+                    ┌──────────────────────┐
+                    │    proposal.md       │ ◀── Problem, scope, impact
+                    └──────────┬───────────┘
+                               │ links to
+              ┌────────────────┼────────────────┐
+              │                │                │
+              ▼                ▼                ▼
+ ┌────────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐
+ │   design.md    │  │  tasks.md    │  │ unification- │
+ │                │  │              │  │ gaps.md      │
+ │  D1–D15       │  │  19 task     │  │              │
+ │  decisions     │  │  sections    │  │  13 gaps     │
+ │  + rationale   │  │  (impl plan)│  │  (all        │
+ └───────┬────────┘  └──────────────┘  │   resolved)  │
+         │                             └──────────────┘
+         │ authoritative specs:
+         ▼
+ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
+ │  specs/                                                  │
+ │                                                         │
+ │  ┌─────────────────────────┐  ┌──────────────────────┐  │
+ │  │ unified-pipeline-schema │  │ shared-inspection-   │  │
+ │  │                         │  │ reader               │  │
+ │  │ • PipelineRun           │  │                      │  │
+ │  │ • Stage, Step           │  │ • Protocol           │  │
+ │  │ • Artifact              │  │ • UnifiedReader      │  │
+ │  │ • ParallelGroup         │  │ • LegacyReader       │  │

+ │  │ • Metadata schema       │  │ • Dependency helpers │  │

+ │                                                         │
+ │  ┌─────────────────────────┐  ┌──────────────────────┐  │

+ │  │                         │  │ extractor            │  │
+ │  │ • Folder layout         │  │                      │  │
+ │  │ • Dual-write strategy   │  │ • AzureDevOpsClient  │  │
+ │  │ • Raw file endpoints    │  │ • Capture plan       │  │
+ │  │ • Issues schema         │  │ • Failure detection  │  │
+ │  └─────────────────────────┘  │ • Normalization      │  │
+ │                               └──────────────────────┘  │
+ │  ┌─────────────────────────┐                            │
+ │  │ ingestion-api           │  ┌──────────────────────┐  │
+ │  │                         │  │ analysis/ (research) │  │
+ │  │ • URL validators        │  │                      │  │
+ │  │ • source_system detect  │  │ • jenkins-inspection │  │
+ │  │ • HTTP 422 for GHA      │  │ • gh-actions-issues  │  │
+ │  └─────────────────────────┘  └──────────────────────┘  │
+ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
+```
diff --git a/openspec/changes/unified-ci-agnostic-schema/design.md b/openspec/changes/unified-ci-agnostic-schema/design.md
